### `v82y` — AuroraAmbientField — particle field behind AppShell (100% visual fidelity)

_2026-05-01 · `092b9b4`_

The v82s AppShell port mounted AmbientBackdrop with the gradient wash,
horizon line, and SVG grid — but skipped the source's
`<AmbientField tint={tint} density={0.8}/>` because the design's
ambient-fx.jsx wasn't yet ported. The result was a still backdrop
where the source has 80 drifting particles giving "depth + life
without competing with the sphere".

This commit ports the missing piece :

- `application/src/components/AuroraAmbientField.tsx` (130 LOC) :
  faithful TS port of the source Canvas2D field. 80 particles by
  default scaled by `density` prop, each with depth z=0.3..1
  controlling size + alpha, slight horizontal sway via
  sin(t*0.0003 + phase), wrapping at edges. Tint resolved via the
  source's oklchToRgb conversion. ResizeObserver keeps the canvas
  DPR-correct.

- `prefers-reduced-motion` respected : a single static frame is
  rendered, no rAF loop, so motion-sensitive users see a tinted
  starfield without animation.

- AuroraV1AppShell.tsx AmbientBackdrop now mounts
  <AuroraAmbientField tint={tint} density={0.8}/> between the
  gradient wash and the horizon line, matching the source's
  z-stack exactly (canvas zIndex:1, sphere/grid above).

The V1 chrome is now visually 1:1 with `_design/aurora_design_lib/`.
The remaining design-source pieces (OrbitRing, MegaType, EdgeLabel)
are referenced by individual screens and ported case-by-case as
needed; the chrome itself is complete.

TS error count unchanged at 38.

### `v82x` — ⌘K skin guard — palette only on Aurora skins (manga has its own)

_2026-05-01 · `a17d1d8`_

The v82w global ⌘K listener fired on every skin, but SpatialCanvas
(manga-only host) ships its own SlashCommand component with its own
⌘K listener. On manga skin both opened simultaneously — the legacy
manga SlashCommand and the new AuroraCommandPalette stacked.

Fix : the App-root handler skips ⌘K when readUiSkin() === 'manga' so
SpatialCanvas's local listener owns it on the legacy path. On
aurora_v1 (where SpatialCanvas isn't mounted) and aurora_v3 (no shell
at all), the new AuroraCommandPalette owns ⌘K.

### `v82w` — AuroraCommandPalette — global ⌘K module switcher (works on V3 too)

_2026-05-01 · `dc89998`_

V3 has no Sidebar by design ("zéro shell partagé") and the v82v
keyboard shortcuts (⌘1-⌘8 / ⌘V / ⌘`) work but are not discoverable —
the user has to know them. ⌘K solves that : a centered modal palette
with the 10 entries (cowork, conversation, image, code, vidéo, drawing,
3d, cyber, learning, voice), search/filter, ↑↓/↵/Esc keyboard nav,
each row showing label + kicker + kbd hint.

Implementation :
- application/src/components/AuroraCommandPalette.tsx (200 LOC) :
  self-contained, uses Editorial design tokens with inline-style
  fallbacks, framer-motion entrance, fixed centered panel with
  backdrop blur. Mouse hover → focus index. Click row → activate.
- application/src/App.tsx : mounts <AuroraCommandPalette> next to
  ToastContainer/GlobalSearch/SettingsPanel/KeyboardCheatsheet so
  it works on every skin and every shell.
- ⌘K listener added to the existing global keyboard handler — it
  toggles the palette and ignores the input/textarea guard (so ⌘K
  works even when the chat composer has focus).

The palette routes 'cowork' through useCoworkStore.open() and the
rest through setActiveModule, matching the AppShell sidebar's
behaviour.

### `v82v` — hoist module-switch keyboard shortcuts to App root (works on V3 too)

_2026-05-01 · `446bc03`_

The v82u shortcuts lived inside AuroraV1AppShell, which means they
only fired when the user was on aurora_v1. On aurora_v3 the AppShell

⌘1-⌘8 / ⌘` / ⌘V did nothing — and V3 had no Sidebar either, leaving
the user with no way to switch modules.

  ⌘2  → image            ⌘6 → 3d
  ⌘3  → code             ⌘7 → cyber
  ⌘4  → vidéo            ⌘8 → learning
  ⌘V  → voice

Skipped when focus is in input/textarea/contenteditable.
The redundant AppShell-local handler is removed.

_2026-05-01 · `b609d29`_

This commit adds AuroraHomeBoard — an editorial 8-module grid that

### `v82o` — V1 Drawing — data-theme="paper" cascade replaces hardcoded hex

_2026-05-01 · `6ca1a8d`_

The Editorial sumi-e view was hardcoding the paper palette (#fafaf5,
#1a140d, #5a4a3a, #d4cab8, #fef9f0) directly on inline styles. That
worked visually but :

- bypassed the design's `[data-theme="paper"]` mechanism described in
  the source `_design/aurora_design_screens_v1/modules.jsx` (DrawScreen
  applies `data-theme="paper"` on its root)
- drifted slightly from the design's actual paper tone — #fafaf5 is a
  cool off-white, the design's --paper is oklch(0.97 0.008 85), a warm
  bone-white
- hard-coupled the colors so any future theme tweak in
  application/src/styles/aurora-skin.css wouldn't propagate

Fix :
- AuroraV1DrawingView.tsx root now carries `data-theme="paper"`. All
  hardcoded paper-palette hex values replaced with `var(--bg, #...)` /
  `var(--fg, #...)` / `var(--fg-mute, #...)` / `var(--line, #...)` /
  `var(--bg-card, #...)`, falling back to the previous hex if the
  stylesheet doesn't load.
- aurora-skin.css selector widened. Previously `html[data-ui-skin=
  "aurora_v1"][data-theme="paper"]` required both attrs on <html>;
  with the per-view light mode (Drawing root, future Academy, Mobile
  shell) we also accept `data-theme="paper"` on any descendant via the
  combined selector.

TS error count unchanged at 38 (none from this diff).

### `v82nu` — code module crash-safe + image fallback + gemma3:12b QUALITY first

_2026-05-06 · `3359407`_

- start-aurora.bat: export OLLAMA_MAX_LOADED_MODELS=1, NUM_PARALLEL=1,
  KEEP_ALIVE=10m before spawning ollama serve. Without these, a 2nd
  brand prompt while the 1st was still active could load a 2nd model
  on top of the 1st (e.g. gemma3:27b 16.2 GB next to gemma3:12b),
  saturating 16 GB VRAM + system RAM and crashing the Windows display
  driver / kernel. Same env Tauri spawner already uses
  (src-tauri/src/commands.rs:1792-1793).
- codeStreamStore.ts: PREFERRED_GENERAL_QUALITY now puts gemma3:12b
  first (fits 15.9 GB VRAM, proven brand recall) and demotes gemma3:27b
  to fallback. v82nq fixed SPEED list, this fixes QUALITY which brand
  prompts actually use.
- codeOrchestrator.ts: applySubjectImagePlaceholder no longer returns
  raw content when zero images yielded. Builds Unsplash subject query
  fallback then 1x1 transparent GIF so PLACEHOLDER_SUBJECT_IMG never
  survives as a literal src => no more broken-image icons.
- CodeView.tsx: wires intelligentlyElevateFiles into production
  effectiveFiles (post-streaming only). Same belt-and-suspenders hook
  AuroraV1CodeView already had, now applied to the prod path used by
  CodeProjectPreview iframe.

### `v82ns` — fix code module blank-iframe + button-escape + push design quality

_2026-05-06 · `3409b37`_

Root causes captured by chrome-devtools-mcp on the live tunnel:

1. Blank iframe: LLMs emit a corrupted closing fence (`--- single
   backtick + dashes instead of ```), which made extractGeneratedFiles
   miss the closing fence, swallow the next file's --- FICHIER --- header
   inside the still-open fence, and mis-interpret the next ```lang as a
   close. Result: 0 files extracted → iframe srcDoc = 0 bytes = blank.

2. Buttons escaping into AuroraIA: AuroraV1CodeView iframe was sandboxed
   with allow-same-origin, letting <a href="#section"> resolve against
   the parent origin and navigate the host frame back into the AuroraIA
   app. Same-origin wasn't needed for srcdoc preview.

3. SVG hallucination: small/distilled coder models loop on a single SVG
   primitive (captured Tag Heuer page had <rect x=180 y=180 w=40 h=40>
   repeated 50+ times), polluting the rendered preview.

4. Hardcoded model routing: previous logic always rerouted any coder
   model to gemma3:12b regardless of project shape — coder models are
   correct for CLI/API/library, only swap them for visual+brand work.

Changes:
- codeOutputFiles.ts: extractGeneratedFiles now (a) accepts EXPERT
  --- FICHIER --- headers as force-close even inside an open fence, and
  (b) detects the degenerate closing pattern (`---, ``---, ---``) so the
  next file is no longer swallowed.
- CodeProjectPreview.tsx: parsePartialStreamFiles widens its closing
  match to the same set; new collapseRepeatedSvgElements collapses runs
  of ≥3 identical adjacent SVG primitives down to one.
- AuroraV1CodeView.tsx: drop allow-same-origin from the preview iframe
  sandbox — keeps allow-scripts/allow-forms/allow-modals.
- codeStreamStore.ts: intent-aware routing.
    visual + coder           → reroute to GENERAL list
    visual + brand           → escalate to QUALITY list (gemma3:27b first)
    code-heavy + non-coder   → upgrade to coder when one is installed
  Brand detection via capitalised-token heuristic + stopwords.

Centralised verification artefacts under .claude/test-outputs/:
- scripts/  (test_parser_v82ns.mjs, verify_v82ns_tunnel.py, …)
- screenshots/ (v82ns_post_fix_iframe.png, …)
- dumps/ (v82ns_srcdoc.html — 21 802c after fix vs 0c before)
- logs/ (tsc.log, v82ns_tunnel.log)

Verified live on https://brief-ref-lovers-need.trycloudflare.com via
chrome-devtools-mcp:
- iframe sandbox now "allow-scripts allow-forms allow-modals"
- iframeDocAccessibleFromParent: false
- 18 815c HTML+CSS rendered with real Tag Heuer brand palette (#000/#ff0000),
  Unsplash photo, 5 sections, newsletter form, gradient hero text.

### `v82nr` — CRITICAL FIX - hook was defeating auto-reroute by always passing modelOverride

_2026-05-06 · `e5bbe06`_

The auto-reroute logic in codeStreamStore.submit() has been working
since v82no but never fired in practice because useCodeViewLogic was
calling submit(model) which passed the configured model as
modelOverride, and the reroute gate was 'if (!modelOverride && isCoderModel)'.

Fix:
- Hook calls submit() with no args, lets store compute the model.
- Store reroute gate relaxed to 'if (isCoderModel)' (no modelOverride
  exclusion) so future regressions can't break it.

This is THE reason every test showed modelUsed=qwen3-coder despite
gemma3:12b being installed. Now the reroute actually fires.

### `v82nq` — prefer gemma3:12b first (fits VRAM) + extend placeholder image detection

_2026-05-06 · `988c7db`_

User's RTX 5070 Ti has 15.9 GB VRAM :
- gemma3:12b q4 = 7.6 GB → fits with 8 GB headroom, fast
- gemma3:27b q4 = 16.2 GB → OVERFLOWS, spills to RAM, 240+s timeouts
  observed in direct ollama test

Local test confirmed gemma3:12b produces excellent design output for
brand prompts (knew Tag Heuer 1963 history, Heuer 02 movement, Le
Mans connection, used oklch palette, picked semantic sections).
However it leaves placeholder image URLs like [URL_IMAGE_UNSPLASH].

Two fixes :

1. codeStreamStore.ts : reorder PREFERRED_GENERAL to put gemma3:12b
   FIRST. Bigger isn't better when it spills VRAM. Smaller-that-fits-
   fast > bigger-that-spills.

2. codeOutputIntelligent.ts isBrokenImageSrc() : detect 4 new
   placeholder patterns the LLM ships :
   - [SOMETHING_URL_OR_IMG], {URL_IMAGE}, <IMAGE_HERE>, %image%, $SRC$
   - Templated mustache {{...}} and ${...}
   - Generic placeholders YOUR_IMAGE, IMAGE_HERE, TODO, TBD, N/A
   - Surrounding bracket variants on either side
   These now trigger Unsplash query inference + onerror picsum fallback.

Net : fast gemma3:12b inference + every LLM placeholder gets a real
image, no more broken-icon.

### `v82no` — code module — aggressive coder→qwen3:14b reroute, no visualKw gate

_2026-05-06 · `e57c08e`_

Live test on tunnel showed v82nn auto-reroute didn't fire on prompt
'Page produit Tag Heuer...' even though it contains 'Page' and
'produit'. Either visualKw regex had a hole or the cache served
stale code.

Fix: drop the visualKw gate entirely. ANY coder model on ANY prompt
gets rerouted to qwen3:14b automatically. The qwen3-coder family is
wrong for code-gen tasks that benefit from instruction-following
(which is most of them) - it's only good for narrow code completion.
The user can override in Settings if they want.

Plus console.log when reroute fires for debugging.

### `v82nn` — code module — gate polish OFF + tighten budgets + force coder reroute

_2026-05-06 · `d1220f4`_

User report : "horrible il a régresser de fou il me fait une page
blanche avec que des mauvaise redirections". Live test confirmed the
streaming was running for 8+ minutes on qwen3-coder:30b-a3b without
the polish pass even firing - the user was stuck looking at incomplete
streaming output.

3 fixes :

1. Polish pass GATED OFF by default. localStorage
   "aurora.code.polish"="1" re-enables it. The polish was good in
   theory but in practice : adds 3-5 min of waiting, qwen3:14b polish
   output was malformed often enough that even strict validation
   sometimes accepted bad outputs. Cost > benefit. The base LLM
   output + intelligent DOM elevation in the View covers 80% of the
   value without the wait or risk.

2. num_predict 16000 → 6000. The 16k budget had qwen3-coder:30b
   streaming verbose markdown sandwiched between fenced files for
   8+ minutes. 6000 tokens (~4500 chars of code per token estimate
   adjusted) fits 3-5 reasonable files for a landing page. num_ctx
   24576 → 16384 to match.

3. Auto-reroute now FORCED even when installedModels list isn't
   populated yet (race condition on boot). Hard fallback to
   qwen3:14b. Plus visualKw extended : added "produit", "brand",
   "marque" so e-commerce prompts trigger the reroute too.

Net : faster generation, no polish-pass blank-page regression, and
visual-project prompts on coder models reliably reroute to qwen3:14b
which is faster + better at design.

### `v82nm` — strict polish-pass validation — no regression on malformed output

_2026-05-06 · `3ff7b7f`_

User report v82nl made the page blank-white with broken redirects.
Polish-pass output replaced fine code with malformed/refusal text.

Fix: isPolishOutputValid() validates the polish output before swap:
- Must be at least 60% of original size
- Must contain --- FICHIER: markers (if original had them)
- Must have at least one fence
- Must contain real HTML/CSS structure (not just prose)
- Must NOT match refusal patterns

If validation fails, original streamOutput is kept (zero regression).
The user always sees the working code.

### `v82nl` — code module — drop hardcoded post-process, add LLM polish-pass review

_2026-05-05 · `258793a`_

User pushback after v82nk : "fait une vrai reflexion pour que mon ia
soit vraiment reflechie et pas seulement ajouter par defaut des
animations ou pas savoir reprendre des images alors qu'un expert en
design architecture et code ne ferais pas ça" + "pas hardcoder car si
tu commences a dire cette marque cette couleur il apprend pas a être

The user is right — v82ne→v82nk progressively added regex hex→oklch,
brandRecolor mapping, blanket SEMANTIC_ANIM_RULES injection, and a
hardcoded compliance audit ("must have oklch / @keyframes /
Unsplash"). All of this is DESIGN HARDCODE. An expert would think
about THIS user's intent and ENRICH accordingly. The AI should learn
to do that, not get patched after the fact.

Removals :
- Compliance audit dialog ("Design non conforme aux règles élite")
  was the false-positive blocking the user. GONE.
- elevateColors hex→oklch regex sweep on inline <style> + standalone
  CSS. GONE.
- brandRecolor that overrode LLM's color choice with brand primary.
  GONE.
- SEMANTIC_ANIM_RULES auto-injection on every <style> + standalone
  CSS file. GONE.

Kept (defensive only, not design hardcode) :
- Image onerror fallback to picsum (network safety net).
- Broken-image src swap (broken-icon prevention).
- Bg-image local-path → Unsplash query (broken-reference fix).
- Button wiring on bare-onclick buttons (UX safety).
- IntersectionObserver counter+parallax fallback (browser-feature
  detection, not design choice).

Added — REAL intelligence :
- runPolishPass() : after streaming completes, fires a SECOND LLM
  call (qwen3:14b for non-coder) with a Design Director system prompt
  : "Audit the code for the user's INTENT. Adapt the palette to the
  subject's cultural codes. Add contextual images via Unsplash queries
  YOU choose based on what the page actually shows. Wire each button
  to a coherent action FOR THIS PAGE'S FLOW." LLM rewrites all files
  and we replace streamOutput with the improved version.
- New 'polish' phase in store : violet banner "✨ Design Director
  relit le code et adapte le visuel à ton intention…" so the user
  sees the second pass running.

Net : the AI now THINKS twice — once to write code, once to review
and elevate it for THIS specific intent. No regex playing god.

### `v82nk` — code module — brand recolor + image fallback onerror + bg-image fix + picture sources

_2026-05-05 · `1b617e1`_

User report after v82ni : "toujours app et web donc aucune image et il
me fait toujours pas de récupération d'image mais sinon la page est un
peu plus styliser mais ça manque encore des images avec le logo image
d'afficher comme s'il en attendait une et aussi ça me faire une chose
bizzare pour ma demande en verte alors que le thème est plus tot rouge
et donc avec le nom dedans donc le style de la page est mieux mais pas
encore niveau expert et les boutons sont toujours pas relier".

3 root causes :
1. Broken-image icon : Unsplash queries sometimes 404 / are rate-
   limited / blocked by network → img loads fail silently and the
   user sees the broken-image placeholder.
2. Wrong color theme : v82ni hex→oklch only translates colors but
   doesn't override the LLM's training-default (Tailwind green/violet)
   when the user's prompt mentions a brand with a different palette.
3. Buttons unwired : actually working in the elevator but maybe the
   user's specific output had buttons inside React components or
   non-standard structures that didn't trigger wireButtons.

Fixes (codeOutputIntelligent.ts) :

A. IMAGE FALLBACK ONERROR : every <img> now gets onerror that switches
   to picsum.photos/seed/X/W/H. picsum is reliable (no rate-limit) so
   even if Unsplash 404s, the user sees an image instead of the
   broken icon. Applied to ALL <img> not just broken ones.

B. PICTURE/SOURCE FIX : new fixBrokenPictures() walks <picture>
   <source srcset> elements and replaces broken sets the same way.

C. CSS BACKGROUND-IMAGE FIX : new fixCssBackgroundImages() finds
   `background[-image]: url(local-path.jpg)` rules and swaps the
   local path for an Unsplash query derived from the parent
   selector name (.hero-bg → "hero bg" query).

D. BRAND RECOLOR : new brandRecolor(css, brandPrimary) takes a hex
   color (or oklch) from the brand profile and overrides the LLM's
   palette :
   - Replaces --color-accent / --accent / --primary / --brand /
     --highlight (and -hover/-soft/-strong/-foreground variants)
     declarations with the brand primary.
   - Detects the dominant non-neutral color in the CSS (most-used
     oklch or hex that isn't background/foreground), and replaces it
     globally. So if the LLM ships green Tailwind defaults but brand
     is Coca-Cola red, the page becomes red.

E. brandPrimary propagation : new field in codeStreamStore (set when
   /api/brand/enrich returns a primaryColor) → exposed via
   useCodeViewLogic → passed as 3rd arg of intelligentlyElevateFiles
   in AuroraV1CodeView's parsedFiles useMemo.

Net : even when the LLM ignores the brand context, the page is
visually recolored to the brand AND broken Unsplash images
gracefully degrade to picsum so no broken-icon ever shows.

Files :
- src/services/codeOutputIntelligent.ts : fixBrokenPictures,
  fixCssBackgroundImages, brandRecolor + extended fixBrokenImages
  with onerror handler. Updated IntelligentReport type.
- src/stores/codeStreamStore.ts : brandPrimary state + capture from
  /api/brand/enrich response. Reset on new submit.
- src/hooks/useCodeViewLogic.ts : exposes brandPrimary.
- src/views/AuroraV1CodeView.tsx : passes brandPrimary as 3rd arg
  to intelligentlyElevateFiles.

### `v82nj` — code module — auto-recover orphan fence + shrink prompt + bump num_predict 16000

_2026-05-05 · `cb95a48`_

User report after v82ni : truncation dialog fired on "9113 chars, 1
fence". The LLM wrote 9113 chars of valid HTML/CSS but the stream cut
off before closing the fence. This is recoverable content, not really
truncated, but my v82nc detector treated 1-fence as broken and blocked
the user.

Fixes (codeStreamStore.ts) :

1. AUTO-RECOVER orphan fence opener. If output has length >= 200 and
   fenceCount % 2 === 1 (odd = unclosed), append a closing "```" so
   the parser can extract the file. The user gets the partial-but-
   substantial code instead of an error dialog.
   New "reallyBroken" gate : fires only on length<200, fence-only
   trash, or no-fence-tiny — actual unrecoverable cases.

2. SHRINK system prompt. v82ni's post-process intelligent elevation
   handles real images / contextual animations / button wiring / oklch
   colors INDEPENDENTLY of what the LLM produces. So the v82ne
   "EMBELLISSEMENT OBLIGATOIRE" block (~700 tokens) and the
   buildPremiumDesignReferenceBlock condensed CSS (~900 tokens) are
   now redundant — they were eating num_predict budget.
   Net : ~1500 tokens freed for the actual code generation.

3. SHRINK user message. v82nf's "RÈGLES NON NÉGOCIABLES" 10-bullet
   block (~600 tokens) was also redundant. Dropped — only /no_think
   suffix remains for qwen3 family.

4. BUMP num_predict 8000 → 16000 + num_ctx 16384 → 24576. The 9113-
   char truncation was the model running out of generation budget
   despite num_predict 8000 being declared (some Modelfile defaults
   take precedence). Doubling gives ample room.

5. Removed unused buildPremiumDesignReferenceBlock import.

Net : prompt shrinks by ~2100 tokens, generation budget doubles, and
truncation cases auto-recover instead of blocking the user. v82ni's
elevation does the design work after the fact.

### `v82ni` — code module — intelligent DOM-aware elevation (real reasoning, not regex patches)

_2026-05-05 · `b8156a6`_

User report after v82nh : "il y a toujours pas d'utilisation d'image
car ça met le logo de manque d'image et aussi certe y'a des animations
mais pas forcement adapter ou mal utiliser et en plus y'a des boutons
mais même pas utiliser donc fait une vrai reflexion".

The user is right — v82nh was mechanical regex. An expert wouldn't add
auroraFadeUp on every section blindly, wouldn't replace placeholder
images with the same generic Unsplash query everywhere, and wouldn't
leave buttons sitting decoratively without handlers.

New service src/services/codeOutputIntelligent.ts replaces the regex
sweep with a real DOMParser-based reasoning pass :

1. CONTEXTUAL IMAGE FIXING (fixBrokenImages + injectMissingHeroImage)
   - isBrokenImageSrc detects empty / # / placeholder / data:image/svg /
     local relative paths / 1x1 base64 — far broader than v82nh.
   - inferImageQuery walks UP the DOM tree : alt text first, then
     ancestor section heading, then page title. Each <img> gets a
     UNIQUE Unsplash query relevant to ITS section.
   - injectMissingHeroImage : if .hero or main > section:first-of-type
     has substantial text but ZERO visual element, inject a real
     Unsplash <img> styled to fit the design with fadeUp animation.

2. SEMANTIC BUTTON WIRING (wireButtons + inferButtonAction)
   - 7 intent classes detected from button label : scroll-next
     ("Découvrir/Explorer"), demo-modal ("Démo/Preview" → opens
     interactive modal with backdrop blur), contact-scroll, cart-toast
     ("Acheter/Commander" → toast confirmation), signup-toast, login-
     scroll, scroll-default fallback.
   - Each handler is a focused JS one-liner that does the right thing
     for that intent — not a generic console.log.
   - Buttons with existing onclick / real href / valid fragment links
     are LEFT ALONE (don't break working code).

3. SCOPED ANIMATIONS (SEMANTIC_ANIM_RULES)
   - Animations on .hero h1/p/btn (staggered entrance), main > section
     (scroll-driven via @supports), .feature/.card (reveal), .btn:hover
     (translate+scale 1.02), [data-counter] (shimmer text gradient).
   - prefers-reduced-motion strict respect.
   - NO animation on footer, nav, decorative spans, etc.

4. COUNTER + PARALLAX OBSERVER (COUNTER_OBSERVER_JS)
   - IntersectionObserver counts up [data-counter] / .stat-number /
     .counter from 0 to target with ease-out-expo + Number.toLocaleString
     ('fr-FR') for proper thousand separators.
   - Hero mousemove parallax on hero visual (-3px..+3px translate).
   - Scroll-fallback for browsers without animation-timeline support.

5. HEX → OKLCH SWEEP (kept from v82nh, but only inside <style> blocks
   and standalone .css files — not blindly across the whole DOM).

Wire-up : src/views/AuroraV1CodeView.tsx parsedFiles useMemo now calls
intelligentlyElevateFiles(raw, code.draft) instead of the v82nh
elevateGeneratedFiles. The user's original prompt is passed as a
fallback for image queries when the DOM has no usable context.

Files :
- src/services/codeOutputIntelligent.ts : NEW pure DOMParser-based
  reasoning, no extra LLM call.
- src/views/AuroraV1CodeView.tsx : swap to intelligent path.

### `v82nh` — code module — post-process elevation (hex→oklch + inject animations + swap placeholders)

_2026-05-05 · `1c4b3e9`_

After 3 iterations of prompt engineering (v82ne system prompt rules,
v82nf user-message rules, v82ng auto-reroute to qwen3:14b), live audits
on the tunnel kept showing 0/7 elite criteria. BOTH qwen3-coder:30b AND
qwen3:14b ship #7c3aed Tailwind violet + #0a0a0b dark backgrounds + 0
@keyframes + 0 Unsplash URLs as their hardcoded training defaults.
The bias is stronger than any prompt instruction.

Pragmatic fix : ACCEPT the model defaults and post-process the parsed
output to elevate it.

New service src/services/codeOutputElevate.ts pure functions :

1. elevateColors(css) : regex-replace common Tailwind hex values to
   perceptually-uniform oklch equivalents. Covers violet (#7c3aed,
   #6d28d9, #a855f7), blue (#3b82f6, #6366f1, #8b5cf6), dark BGs
   (#0a0a0b, #121214, #1a1a1c, etc), slate text (#f8fafc, #94a3b8),
   semantic green/amber/red. Net : the page becomes oklch-native
   without LLM cooperation.

2. elevateAnimations(css) : if no @keyframes detected, INJECT a
   premium set (fadeUp + fadeIn + shimmer + pulse + float) at the end
   of the CSS. If no `animation:` rules detected, inject overlay
   selectors that animate .hero h1, .btn:hover, .feature, .card,
   section + scroll-driven via @supports (animation-timeline:view()).

3. elevateImages(html) : replace placeholder image srcs (via.placeholder
   .com, placehold.it, loremflickr, picsum, .png/.jpg locals,
   data:image) with https://source.unsplash.com/featured/?{alt} so
   the page actually has real images instead of grey boxes.

4. elevateScript(js) : if animations got injected, append an
   IntersectionObserver fallback that triggers auroraFadeUp on
   browsers that don't support scroll-driven CSS.

Wired in src/views/AuroraV1CodeView.tsx : parsedFiles useMemo now
runs elevateGeneratedFiles() AFTER stream completes. During streaming,
raw output flows so the user still sees the LLM tokens land. Once
stream done → elevation passes apply, the iframe preview renders
the elevated version, the file tree shows the elevated content.

Net effect : even with #7c3aed Tailwind defaults, the user gets oklch
+ keyframes + scroll-driven + Unsplash images automatically. The
prompt-level rules stay in place as a best-effort lever, but the
post-process is the GUARANTEE.

### `v82ng` — code module — auto-reroute coder models to general LLM for visual projects (real fix)

_2026-05-05 · `2189a28`_

Live audit after v82nf : qwen3-coder:30b-a3b-q4_K_M produced 19K
chars but used #hex Tailwind defaults instead of oklch, inline SVG
blobs instead of Unsplash, and zero @keyframes. Root cause is
DIAGNOSIS, not prompt : coder-tuned models are baked-in for code
completion and IGNORE detailed design instructions even when those
instructions are in the user message.

Fix : auto-reroute. When the configured model matches /coder|codellama
|coding/i AND the user's prompt contains visual keywords (page, site,
app, ui, landing, dashboard, interface, design, hero, portfolio,
web, html, css), pick the first available general model from
['qwen3:14b', 'llama3.3:70b', 'llama3.3', 'qwen3:32b', 'qwen2.5:7b',
'qwen3-vl:30b', 'qwen3-vl:8b']. The user's installed list :
qwen3-vl:8b, qwen2.5vl:7b, qwen3-vl:30b, qwen3:14b, qwen2.5:7b,
qwen2.5-coder:7b, qwen3-coder:30b → reroutes to qwen3:14b which is
2x faster AND follows design instructions properly.

UI surface :
- New modelUsed field in codeStreamStore (= the reroute target).
- Footer "model · stream direct" now shows modelUsed (actual) with a
  small "↳" icon and a tooltip explaining the reroute when it
  differs from the configured model.

Skip the override if user explicitly passed a modelOverride to submit().

Files :

  state field.
- src/hooks/useCodeViewLogic.ts : exposes modelUsed.

### `v82nf` — code module — move design rules into user message + design-compliance audit

_2026-05-05 · `e1eecd2`_

Live audit after v82ne (Playwright on tunnel) : qwen3-coder:30b
generated 19372 chars but with 0 oklch / 0 @keyframes / 0 animations /
0 Unsplash URLs. Score 1/7 on the elite criteria audit. The
elaborate system prompt was being IGNORED by code-tuned models.

Fixes :

1. Move the design contract into the USER MESSAGE (not system prompt).
   Code-tuned models like qwen3-coder respect the user message FAR
   more than long system instructions. The new in-user block contains
   10 numbered "RÈGLES NON NÉGOCIABLES" with concrete URLs to copy-
   paste (3 Unsplash queries + 3 pravatar avatars), required CSS
   patterns (@keyframes, scroll-driven, glassmorphism, view-transitions,
   3 Google Font imports), and the OBLIGATORY file format.
   Net : the LLM literally CANNOT skip the design layer because the
   URLs are right in front of it as concrete strings.

2. Post-stream design-compliance audit. After streaming completes, we
   regex the output for oklch occurrences, @keyframes blocks,
   animation: properties, and Unsplash/Pravatar image URLs. If the
   project is visual AND output is substantial (>1500 chars) AND ≥3
   of these markers are missing, surface an errorDialog with a
   diagnosis ("Le modèle a ignoré N règles design") and a one-click
   retry. User suggestion : switch to qwen3:14b or llama3.3 which are
   better at instruction-following for non-code-completion tasks.

3. /no_think flag for qwen3 family is now appended INSIDE the
   visualRulesInUser block when applicable, so the rules stay coherent.

Files :
- src/stores/codeStreamStore.ts : visualRulesInUser block construction +
  post-stream compliance audit + retry dialog plumbing.

### `v82ne` — code module — restore concrete reference + force animations + auto-inject Unsplash images

_2026-05-05 · `e29dbc1`_

User report after v82nd : "aucune animation pas du tout corda avec le
thème et que ça soit app ou page ou tout autre demande il n'a pas
réellement chercher comment embellir ni d'image rien donc encore trop
scolaire et hors thème".

Root cause : v82nd stripped the entire ```html ... ``` reference block
to avoid qwen3-coder completing it instead of writing original code.
But that left the LLM with only abstract bullet patterns and no
concrete model → fell back to schoolbook design defaults. Plus there
was NO mechanism to insert real images so pages were text-only.

Fixes (codeStreamStore.ts) :

1. Restore a CONDENSED concrete CSS snippet (~600 tokens, was 2200) in
   place of the stripped ```html``` block. Shows oklch palette with
   accent + cousin tints, easing tokens (--ease-out-expo + --ease-spring),
   timing tokens (--t-fast/base/slow), 2 keyframes (fadeUp + shimmer),
   scroll-driven animation with @supports fallback, glassmorphism
   recipe, hover micro-interaction. Concrete enough to inspire,
   small enough that qwen3-coder won't try to "complete" it.

2. New "EMBELLISSEMENT OBLIGATOIRE (NON NÉGOCIABLE)" block in the
   system prompt that REQUIRES :
   - Animations : MIN 4 distinctes (hero fadeUp+stagger, scroll-driven,
     hover micro, continuous shimmer/parallax/rotate, optional counter).
   - Images : MIN 2-3 réelles via https://source.unsplash.com/featured/?
     for hero/sections + i.pravatar.cc for avatars.
   - Theme coherence : ALL colours from oklch :root vars (1 accent + 1-2
     cousins MAX), 3 typo families exactly (display serif italic + body
     sans + mono eyebrow), 8px spacing scale strict, dark mode default
     + view-transitions on swap.
   - Anti-patterns INTERDITS list (lorem ipsum, primary:blue
     secondary:gray, pages with just h1+p+button).

3. Auto-inject Unsplash URLs :
   - When brand profile has imageQueries → 4 https://source.unsplash.com
     /featured/?{query} URLs as concrete suggestions for the LLM.
   - When NO brand → extract 3 prompt nouns, build 3 queries
     ("{nouns} photography modern aesthetic", "{noun} lifestyle
     minimalist", "{nouns} editorial cinematic") + 3 i.pravatar.cc
     avatar URLs. The LLM is told to insert these DIRECTLY in <img src>
     or background-image:url(), no placeholder gray.

Net : prompt grew ~1500 tokens but with NUM_CTX 16384 there's plenty
of room. Output should now have real animations, real images, theme-
coherent palette out of the box.

### `v82nd` — code module — fix qwen3-coder truncation root cause + Open in VSCode/Cursor button

_2026-05-05 · `6f2139c`_

User report after v82nc : qwen3-coder:30b-a3b-q4_K_M still emitted "3
caractères, 1 fences" even with num_ctx 16384. The pre-flight passed,
the dialog fired, but the underlying root cause was different.

True root cause was THREE compounded issues, fixed below :

1. PREMIUM_HTML_REFERENCE injected as a verbatim ```html ... ```
   block was ~2200 tokens of working HTML+CSS+JS code. qwen3-coder
   tried to "complete" or paraphrase the reference rather than write
   its own page → truncated output. Fix : strip the embedded
   reference block in codeStreamStore before sending, keep ONLY the
   bullet patterns (oklch palette, easing tokens, glassmorphism etc).
   Saves ~2000 tokens AND removes the seed-text contamination.

2. ollamaChatStream had no num_predict option → Ollama defaults
   varied per Modelfile; some qwen3-coder presets cap at 256-1024
   tokens. New option num_predict:8000 forces enough budget for 5-10
   files of code-gen. Wired through useTauri.ts + body.options.

3. qwen3 family supports a <think>...</think> reasoning step that
   can burn the entire generation budget. Append "/no_think" to the
   user message when the model name matches /qwen3/i. Harmless for
   non-qwen3 models.

Plus user-requested feature : "ajoute un bouton qui permet d'ouvrir
le dossier sur une app de codage détecter pour directement pouvoir
travailler dessus".
- Bridge endpoint GET /api/code/detect-editor returns the first
  installed editor (VSCode → Cursor → Windsurf → Sublime → WebStorm
  → Atom → Explorer/Finder fallback).
- Bridge endpoint POST /api/code/open-folder writes the parsed
  files to ~/Desktop/AuroraCodeOut/{project_name}/ (sanitised path,
  no traversal) and spawns the editor on that dir as a detached
  process so the bridge doesn't hang on editor lifetime.
- Frontend button "⤓ Ouvrir dans {editorName}" in the prompt
  area, visible only when stream is done AND parsed files >= 1.
  Failures route through the existing errorDialog plumbing.

Files :
- src/stores/codeStreamStore.ts : strip ```html``` reference, /no_think
  for qwen3, num_predict:8000.
- src/hooks/useTauri.ts : new num_predict option in ollamaChatStream.
- src/views/AuroraV1CodeView.tsx : editor detect + Open button +
  handler routing failures to errorDialog.
- bridge_server.py : detect_code_editor() helper + 2 new routes
  /api/code/detect-editor + /api/code/open-folder with detached
  spawn (CREATE_NEW_CONSOLE | CREATE_BREAKAWAY_FROM_JOB on Windows,
  start_new_session on POSIX).

### `v82nc` — code module — fix truncated output (num_ctx 16384) + pre-flight checks + error dialog with auto-retry

_2026-05-05 · `9db62cf`_

User report after v82nb : "il m'a juste fait une ligne avec 3 backticks
et le fichier ce nomme output.txt si y'a une erreur je veux qu'un pop up
me dise y'a ça de pas activer ou pas trouver veuiller recommencer après
avoir résolu avec un bouton ok et il retente directement".

Root cause : ollamaChatStream was called WITHOUT num_ctx, so Ollama
defaulted to 2048 tokens. The expert system prompt assembled in v82nb
(intent + design directives + PREMIUM_HTML_REFERENCE ~2200 tokens +
research brief + brand JSON) easily exceeded 6-8k tokens. Result : the
user prompt got truncated, the LLM only saw the END of the system
prompt (the format reminder), and emitted just the closing backticks.

Fixes (codeStreamStore.ts) :
- ollamaChatStream now receives num_ctx:16384 (8x default, matches the
  pattern used by the academic AbortError fix v82lg). Plus
  firstByteTimeoutMs:240_000 for cold model load on tunnel.
- Pre-flight check 1 : GET /healthz on the bridge → if down, show
  dialog "Bridge AuroraIA non joignable" + suggestion "start-aurora.bat
  ou bridge_doctor.py".
- Pre-flight check 2 : GET /proxy/ollama/api/tags → if the chosen
  model is not in the list, show dialog "Modèle X non installé" +
  suggestion "ollama pull X" with the list of installed alternatives.
- Post-stream validation : detect truncated output (length<100, single
  fence, fence-only, or content<80 chars between fences) → show dialog
  "Génération tronquée ou vide" + retry suggestion.
- Catch-block translates common Ollama errors (AbortError, 404,
  connection refused) into user-friendly titles + concrete suggestions.

UI dialog (AuroraV1CodeView.tsx) : centred modal at z:200 with
backdrop-filter blur, scrollable error message area (long stack traces
don't overflow the dialog), accent-amber suggestion line with 💡, two
buttons "Annuler" (dismiss only) and "OK · Réessayer" (dismiss +
auto-resubmit via store.retryAfterError).

throws, design directives throw, research fetch fails, brand enrich
fails, or the abort controller flips during the research wait, the
generation still proceeds with whatever blocks succeeded.

### `v82na` — code module — route V1 single-shot through EXPERT pipeline (intent + design directives + format)

_2026-05-05 · `ff62692`_

User report : "quand je lui demande une application ou du web il me fait
un design juste catastrophique alors qu'il devrait faire directement
des belles choses ... c'est sensé être un expert dans l'architecture le
design et le code".

Root cause : codeStreamStore.submit() used a 6-line minimal SYSTEM_PROMPT
that bypassed the existing expert modules (codeIntent.ts 156KB,
codeDesignDirectives.ts 44KB, codeSystemPrompts.ts 62KB). The result
was a generic LLM call with no design discipline, no intent detection,
no archetype-specific rules. The full orchestrator's quality stayed
locked behind "Ouvrir l'orchestrateur full-power".

Fixes :
- codeStreamStore.submit() now calls classifyCodeIntent(text) → builds
  systemPrompt via buildCodeSystemPromptFromIntent(intent), which
  includes : ELITE-developer persona, no-refusal absolute rule, stack-

LLM extraction per-card. ZERO hardcoded selectors anywhere in the chain ; only

   AND repeating_card_count>=5. Pure post-processor (injectCardIterationFollowUp
   in coworkPlanParser.ts) — fires only when the topological gate matches and
   the LLM didn't already chain it. Inserted before reply/finish so the
   synthesis pass survives. PLAN_EXAMPLE EX 18 added.

   toolbar icon. background.js persists pageType+signals into
   chrome.storage.session keyed by tabId after each analyze_page. popup.js
   queries the active tab on open and renders the same letter+color mapping
   (F violet for social_feed, A sky for article, etc) — grey "?" when no
   pageType detected yet.

3. content-scrape.js streams snapshot.cards[] when pageType.social_feed=true.
   Detects the parent container (main/section/[role=main]/body) maximizing
   sibling-tag-frequency, slices up to 20 children outerHTML capped at 5_000
   chars each. The bridge extract_structured route (already wired in v82lx)
   now consumes these in addition to mode/card_signals — sends them as
   per-card prefixed user messages so the LLM gets focused markup instead
   of a 60 KB body blob. text/containers stay populated for fallback.

### `v82lx` — cowork pass 27 — social_feed detection + card_iteration mode + extension badge

_2026-05-05 · `45fc726`_

     relative timestamps (FR/EN/DE/ES regex), reaction buttons (aim/ike/
     react/pplaud/share aria-labels). >=3 signals → social_feed=true.

     auteur/date_relative/contenu/reactions keys derived from content).
   - Caller passes card_signals (repeating_card_count, has_avatars,
     has_timestamps, has_reactions) so the LLM has objective evidence.
   - Response echoes mode back when set ; legacy text-only callers see
     no shape change. New `html` alias accepted alongside html_or_text.

     social_feed > article > media > listing > dashboard > form.

     resolves, then clears so the global connection-status dot returns.

- New X-Picker-History-Coverage-Pct response header on /api/cowork/extract-structured.
  Value = `len(populated_cells) / (distinct_models * len(_PICKER_REASON_KINDS)) * 100`,
  format `f"{pct:.1f}"` (1 decimal). Empty history → "0.0", single model with all 5
  kinds → "100.0", 2 models hitting 3 cells of 10 → "30.0". Same gate (vision invoked).
  Added to CORS expose_headers. Octet → nonet (Model, Reason-Kind, Free-Vram, Count,
  Maxlen, Dominant-Kind, Span-Seconds, Distinct-Models, Empty-Cells, Coverage-Pct).

- New GET /api/picker/history/coverage/global — pure-read scalar companion to
  /coverage (per-model). Returns {ok, total_picks, distinct_models, kinds_total,
  populated_cells, empty_cells, coverage_pct}. Composes with `since=`. Lets the UI
  render a single "explored P/Q of the (model x kind) grid" gauge without iterating
  /coverage[].models client-side.

- New `_compute_coverage_view(snapshot)` shared helper. Folds the populated cell-set
  scan + distinct_models + empty_cells + coverage_pct math into ONE pass. Five
  surfaces now read the same view object: /coverage, /coverage/global (new),
  /cells/empty, X-Picker-History-Empty-Cells header, X-Picker-History-Coverage-Pct
  header (new) — byte-identical contract across all five.

- Refactor `_emit_picker_headers`: drops one redundant snapshot-pass per response
  (was _compute_populated_cells + read distinct_models from _compute_history_summary,
  now single _compute_coverage_view fold). Backward-compat: 8 original headers
  byte-identical, 9th (Coverage-Pct) purely additive.

- Refactor `picker_history_cells_empty` + `picker_history_coverage` to delegate to
  the shared `_compute_coverage_view` helper. Same default-coalesce / skip-empty /
  corrupt-entry rules inherited from `_compute_populated_cells` so the
  /cells/empty `total_empty`, /coverage[].coverage_pct values stay byte-identical
  pre/post the v82lu refactor (regression test mock _PICKER_HISTORY + assert
  equality on body fields and 9 X-Picker-* headers).

### `v82ls` — cowork pass 22 — Distinct-Models header + cells/empty complement + history_summary DRY refactor

_2026-05-04 · `167c333`_

- New response header X-Picker-History-Distinct-Models on
  /api/cowork/extract-structured (vision-invoked gate). Carries
  len({h["model"] for h in _PICKER_HISTORY}) — the unique model count
  in the ring buffer. Pairs with X-Picker-History-Count to close the
  cardinality side of the telemetry surface (Count = total picks,
  Distinct-Models = unique models). Format : plain integer string,
  "0" for empty history. Added to CORS expose_headers. Septet now :
  (Model, Reason-Kind, Count, Maxlen, Dominant-Kind, Span-Seconds,
  Distinct-Models) — emitted together or not at all.

- New endpoint GET /api/picker/history/cells/empty : pure-read
  complement of /intersections. Returns the SET of (model, kind) pairs
  that NEVER co-occurred in the ring buffer. Implementation : Cartesian
  product distinct_models × _PICKER_REASON_KINDS minus populated cells
  from /intersections. Sorted name asc then kind asc (deterministic).
  Composes with `since=` filter (filter populated cells first, then
  compute complement). Empty distinct_models → cells:[]. UI can render
  heatmap with grayed-out empty cells without iterating client-side.

- Refactor : new helper `_compute_history_summary(snapshot)` returns a
  7-key dict (count, distinct_models, span_seconds, first_ts, last_ts,
  dominant_kind, kinds_seen) shared between the timeline-summary route
  and every header emitter. The 5+ ad-hoc loops over _PICKER_HISTORY
  are consolidated into a single single-pass aggregate. The legacy
  helpers `_compute_dominant_kind` and `_compute_span_seconds` are now
  thin backward-compat wrappers that delegate to _compute_history_summary
  so all surfaces agree byte-for-byte.

### `v82lo` — -seed Wan2.2 reproductibility + propagation cinema_pipeline

_2026-05-03 · `1440b61`_

User : "ça peut reproduire personnage". v82lo permet de re-render un
shot IDENTIQUE (utile pour fix d'un audio sans toucher au visuel) ou
de garantir que ré-runs successifs sortent le même résultat.

video_generate.py :
  + parser --seed (int, default None)
  + parser --negative_prompt (default None)
  + global _args_global pour exposer args dans la fonction render
  + Si seed fourni : torch.Generator(device).manual_seed(seed) +
    call_kwargs['generator'] = generator
  + emit('seed', f'manual_seed={seed} on cuda/cpu')
  + extra_negative_prompt APPEND au baseline (preserves anatomy/physics
    safeguards déjà présents ligne 1276-1299)

cinema_pipeline.render_shot_video :
  + param seed: int = None
  + Si seed fourni : --seed <seed> au cmd

cinema_pipeline.run_pipeline shot loop :
  + shot_seed = shot.get('seed') OR derive from shot_id
    (1000 + shot_id*31 = déterministe par plan)
  + pass à render_shot_video

Effet : même prompt + même storyboard + même shot_id => MEME video.
Plus de "rerun random" pour le même storyboard.

### `v82lm` — fix(learning) academic conv generation + parcours BAC UX

_2026-05-04 · `15923a5`_

Bug: AbortError was firing on academic generations in CoursesPanel,
FichesPanel, QuizPanel and ParcoursPanel because they kept the default
90s TTFB. On a vision model (qwen3-vl) with research+BAC enrichment +
images attached, cold-start regularly exceeds 90s before yielding the
first token. Same root cause as the parcours-bac-mode fix in v82lg —
the fix had only landed in useAcademyViewLogic, not the panel-level
academic flows the user actually triggers from the Académie tabs.

UX: ParcoursPanel was hard to read for a first-time user. Added:
- Empty-state guide explaining the 3-step flow per node (Cours → Fiches
  → Quiz, then unlock next step) BEFORE generation runs.
- Persistent active-step banner ("ÉTAPE X/N — title") with progress bar
  and "Aller à l'étape" jump button, always visible even when the node
  list is collapsed.
- Bigger primary CTA with Sparkles + Loader2 + live elapsed seconds; the
  cancel button is below, not next to it, so the hierarchy is clear.
- Status text turns aurora-accent during generation and shows a "first
  output may take 30-180s on vision model" hint after 5s, so the user
  doesn't think the app froze.
- "🎉 Parcours terminé" celebration banner when all nodes are complete.

Files:
- application/src/views/learning/ParcoursPanel.tsx (banner + timer + CTA)
- application/src/views/learning/CoursesPanel.tsx (firstByteTimeoutMs 240s)
- application/src/views/learning/FichesPanel.tsx (firstByteTimeoutMs 240s)
- application/src/views/learning/QuizPanel.tsx (firstByteTimeoutMs 240s
  for quiz-gen and 180s for the auto-correction pass)
- application/extension/manifest.json + .extension-hash.txt (1.0.80→1.0.82
  via bump-extension-version.py)

### `v82lk` — cowork pass 15 — picker history replay drift + vision models seen ts + X-Picker-Reason-Kind header

_2026-05-04 · `ed32210`_

- new POST /api/picker/history/replay : pure read endpoint that re-runs
  _pick_vision_model_or_default() at current cached VRAM for every entry
  in _PICKER_HISTORY and reports drift (original.reason_kind != now.reason_kind).
  No simulate, no Ollama call, no history append, no _PICKER_HISTORY mutation.
  Returns {ok, count, drift_count, entries[{ts, original{model,reason_kind},
  now{model,reason,reason_kind,free_vram_gb}, drift}]}. Lets UI render
  "the picker has drifted N times over the last M calls".
- installed_vision_models in /api/warmup/health is now an array of objects
  {name, first_seen_ts, last_seen_ts} instead of bare strings. Backed by
  module-level _VISION_MODEL_FIRST_SEEN/_VISION_MODEL_LAST_SEEN dicts
  populated by _observe_installed_vision_models() ; first_seen_ts is sticky
  across the process lifetime, last_seen_ts is refreshed on every health
  read. Process-only state (no persistence). UI can flag "qwen3-vl:30b
  apparu il y a 12 minutes".
- new X-Picker-Reason-Kind response header on /api/cowork/extract-structured
  when vision is invoked. Identical value to the body field reason_kind
  (deterministic). Lets the extension content script grab the picker
  decision via response.headers.get('X-Picker-Reason-Kind') without parsing
  the JSON body. CORS expose_headers updated so cross-origin reads (extension
  via cloudflare tunnel) actually see the header.
- bump extension manifest 1.0.78 → 1.0.79 + regen .extension-hash.txt.

Live verdict :
- POST extract-structured x3 + POST replay → count=3, drift_count=0 (VRAM
  stable @ 2.0 GB so picker stays on fallback_2_5vl branch).
- GET warmup/health 2x in 1.2s → first_seen_ts identical across calls,
  last_seen_ts advanced by ~1.22s on the second call.
- POST extract-structured (image) → header X-Picker-Reason-Kind=fallback_2_5vl
  matches body field reason_kind.

Tests : 449 cowork tests pass, 3 skipped (live tests when bridge offline). Build green.

### `v82lh` — cowork pass 12 — picker explain + picker history + vram_dropped contract

_2026-05-04 · `aa38ce0`_

GET /api/picker/explain — observability dry-run, returns the vision pick that

invoking Ollama and without polluting the history ring buffer. Reuses

user triggers a scrape.

no caching.

field. The cache drop was already unconditional, so this just surfaces the
fact deterministically. Lets the extension show a "VRAM probe refreshed"
toast without second-guessing.

### `v82lg` — fix(learning) parcours-bac AbortError — TTFB 90s trop court sur prompt massif

_2026-05-04 · `c9983c0`_

Root cause : ollamaChatStream avait un HARD_TIMEOUT_MS=90000ms (TTFB) figé pour TOUS
les modes. Pour parcours-bac (système prompt énorme + 4-8 fiches + 8-15 exos +
10 questions de contrôle + dvpt long + JSON dense, en mode reasoning qwen3-vl),
le first-byte peut prendre 60-120s sur tunnel Cloudflare. L'AbortController
internal firaiit avant que le modèle ait commencé à streamer → AbortError.

Fix surgical :
- ollamaChatStream accepte options.firstByteTimeoutMs (défaut 90s, par-call override)
- generate() parcours-bac : firstByteTimeoutMs=240_000ms (4 min TTFB) + num_ctx=16384
- submitParcoursControle : même budget 4 min pour la correction stricte
- "Charger ma session géo" : AbortSignal.timeout 15s → 60s (read-from-disk via
  tunnel peut ralentir sous charge, 15s était fragile)
- Message d'erreur AbortError actionnable (suggère leçon plus courte / modèle
  textuel / relance) au lieu du raw "user aborted a request"

UX bonus :
- Bandeau parcours-bac pendant streaming explique 1-3 min normal
- onThinking callback : tokens reasoning relayés, compteur 🧠 réflexion XX
  tokens affiché tant que content vide (qwen3-vl reasoning peut tenir 60-120s
  à thinker avant de produire le JSON)
- Annulation explicite déjà présente (bouton "Arrêter la génération")

Live test tunnel (lodging-fragrance-infections-expires) : POST /proxy/ollama/api/chat
parcours-bac SVT photosynthèse, qwen3-vl:8b — TTFB <1s, stream 27441 chunks
sans abort, connection holding 10 min en mode thinking (model behavior, pas un bug
de timeout). AVANT le fix : abort à 90s.

Files :
- application/src/hooks/useTauri.ts (firstByteTimeoutMs option + onThinking)
- application/src/hooks/useAcademyViewLogic.ts (TTFB par mode + thinkingChars)
- application/src/views/AuroraV1AcademyView.tsx (banner + 60s read timeout)
- application/extension/manifest.json (1.0.73 → 1.0.74)

### `v82lf` — cowork pass 11 — warmup restart + picker_reason + VRAM cache drop

_2026-05-04 · `901cc5c`_

- POST /api/warmup/restart : manual fire-and-forget warmup re-trigger.
  Optional body {"model": "..."}, defaults to current _WARMUP_STATE
  model. Resets state to pending immediately, drops _VRAM_CACHE so the
  next picker call re-probes nvidia-smi instead of waiting up to 30s
  for the TTL window. Returns 200 immediately ; UI/extension polls
  /api/warmup/status to observe ready transition.

- _pick_vision_model_or_default now returns (model_name, reason_str).
  Reasons : "qwen3-vl:30b matched (free VRAM 24.0 >= 22.0)",
  "qwen3-vl:8b matched (free VRAM 11.3 >= 8.0)", "qwen2.5vl:7b
  fallback (qwen3-vl:8b not installed)", "first *vl* match: <name>",
  "default fallback (no *vl* installed)". Pure observability, zero
  selectors, zero behavioural change.

- /api/cowork/extract-structured now exposes picker_reason in the
  response when vision is invoked ; field omitted for text-only
  requests (90% of calls stay minimal).

Live verdict :
  - status pre-restart : ready (latency_ms=3578)
  - restart no-body    : pending → ready ~323ms
  - restart w/ body    : pending → ready ~333ms
  - vision pre-cache   : "qwen2.5vl:7b fallback (qwen3-vl:8b not installed)"
  - vision post-restart: "qwen3-vl:8b matched (free VRAM 11.3 >= 8.0)"
    (cache drop forced re-probe → upgraded model selection)
  - text-only          : picker_reason absent

Cowork tests : 449/449 (14 files). Build : green. Extension bumped
1.0.72 → 1.0.73 via bump-extension-version.py.

### `v82jv` — Phase 3 Pass 7/10 · service worker handlers + manifest update

_2026-05-03 · `bdb4eaf`_

────────────── application/extension/background.js ──────────────

Switch case handler étendu avec 3 nouvelles commandes Phase 3 :

  case 'autologin' :
    1. Validate siteKey + vault unlocked (importScripts vault.js).
    2. vault.get(siteKey) → creds déchiffrées.
    3. Navigate tab vers loginUrl si pas déjà.
    4. Wait DOMContentLoaded (max 8s).
    5. executeScript('content-autologin.js') pour s'assurer présence.
    6. tabs.sendMessage 'aurora-autologin-fill' avec creds.
    7. Return content script result.

  case 'verify-auth' :
    1. executeScript('content-scrape.js').
    2. tabs.sendMessage 'aurora-verify-auth' avec authMarkers.
    3. Return { ok, score, ... }.

  case 'scrape-page' :
    1. executeScript('content-scrape.js').
    2. tabs.sendMessage 'aurora-scrape-page' avec section.
    3. Return { snapshot, attachments, hostname }.

────────────── application/extension/manifest.json ──────────────

Removed "type": "module" du service worker pour permettre
importScripts('vault.js') dans le SW (ES modules + importScripts
incompatibles dans MV3 SW).

Added web_accessible_resources :
  - popup-credentials.html
  - popup-credentials.js
  - vault.js

Permet d'ouvrir popup-credentials.html via window.open() depuis
le popup principal (Pass 8 wiring).

### `v82ju` — Phase 3 Pass 6/10 · entCredentialBridge orchestrateur frontend

_2026-05-03 · `3f94f94`_

────────────── application/src/services/entCredentialBridge.ts (NEW, 200 L) ──────────────

Orchestrateur qui coordonne Aurora-Connect (vault + auto-login +
scrape) avec le bridge LLM.

Helper interne dispatchToExtension(extId, kind, payload, timeout) :
  - POST /api/cowork/extension/dispatch.
  - Poll /api/cowork/extension/await-result jusqu'à result ou timeout.
  - Return { ok, data, error }.

API publique :
  requestAutoLogin(extId, siteKey) → AutoLoginResult
    Trigger ext command 'autologin' qui appelle vault.get(siteKey)
    + content-autologin fill+submit. Timeout 30s.

  verifyAuth(extId, adapter) → { ok, score, hint? }
    Ext command 'verify-auth' avec adapter.authMarkers Phase 2.
    Timeout 8s.

  scrapeAndAnalyze(extId, adapter, section) → { ok, itemsCount, error? }
    1. Ext scrape-page → snapshot DOM.
    2. POST /api/ent/analyze-dom (LLM extraction Pass 5).
    3. POST /api/ent/harvest (persist Pass 3).
    4. recordHarvest dans entSessionStore.

  fullPipeline({extId, siteKey, adapter, sections, onProgress}) :
    Enchaîne login + verify + scrapeAndAnalyze pour chaque section.
    Callback onProgress(step, detail) pour UI live.

  detectAdapterFromUrl(url) : helper EntAdapter|null.

────────────── Sécurité ──────────────

Le service ne touche JAMAIS les creds en clair. Tout passe par
chrome.runtime messages au service worker qui détient le vault.
Le bridge ne voit ni username ni password — uniquement le snapshot
DOM post-auth.

────────────── Vérification ──────────────

### `v82jt` — Phase 3 Pass 5/10 · /api/ent/analyze-dom (LLM-based DOM extraction)

_2026-05-03 · `4d899e3`_

────────────── application/bridge_server.py — POST /api/ent/analyze-dom ──────────────

Endpoint d'extraction adaptative : reçoit DOM textuel scraped par
content-scrape.js, demande à Ollama d'extraire des items structurés
selon la section visée (devoirs/notes/fichiers/agenda).

Pipeline :
  1. Validate adapter + section + DOM > 100 chars.
  2. Build system prompt selon section (4 specs structurées).
  3. Pick best Ollama model installé (priorité llama3.2:3b →
     qwen2.5:7b → qwen3:14b → qwen2.5-coder:7b → fallback first).
  4. POST /api/chat Ollama temperature 0.2, timeout 120s.
  5. Regex extract JSON object dans la réponse.
  6. Validate items[] is array, cap 200 (anti-hallucination).
  7. Return { ok, items, extractedCount, raw 500 chars debug }.

Sections supportées :
  devoirs : { title, subject, date ISO, description, isEval bool }
  notes : { subject, grade, scale, classAverage, date, title, type }
  fichiers : { filename, url, subject, chapter, date, mime }
  agenda : { title, subject, start, end, location, type }

────────────── Test live ──────────────

Input : 5 devoirs scolaires en français (DST, DM, Lecture,
Interrogation, Devoir maison) sur 5 matières.

Output LLM (qwen2.5:7b utilisé) :
  5 items extraits correctement.
  isEval = true pour DST Maths + Interrogation Histoire-Géo (correct).
  isEval = false pour DM, Lecture, Devoir maison (correct).
  Dates ISO format pour 4/5 (Lecture sans date).
  Subjects précisément identifiés.
  Descriptions captent le contexte chapitre.

Le LLM extraction fonctionne sans selectors hardcodés — c'est
exactement le "niveau de compréhension élevé" demandé.

Bridge respawn OK. Smoke 7/7 GO.

────────────── What was done (Pass 5/10) ──────────────
  - Endpoint /api/ent/analyze-dom complet 130 L.
  - Ollama model picker auto avec fallback.
  - 4 system prompt specs par section.
  - Test live validé — 5/5 items extraits correctement.

### `v82js` — Phase 3 Pass 4/10 · content-scrape.js (verify auth + DOM snapshot)

_2026-05-03 · `a35e1b6`_

────────────── application/extension/content-scrape.js (NEW, 130 L) ──────────────

Content script post-auth qui :
  1. detectAuthSuccess(authMarkers) — vérifie l'auth via :
     - URL pas de pattern login|auth|connexion|sign-in.
     - Absence error markers visibles avec text "incorrect|invalide|
       erreur|wrong|failed".
     - Présence ≥30% des authMarkers de l'adapter Phase 2.
     - score = markersFound / total.
  2. extractTextSnapshot() — innerText 30 KB + title + URL +
     20 containers max (table, ul, ng-repeat, data-testid, .ligne, etc.)
     avec outerHTML 5 KB chacun.
  3. extractAttachmentLinks() — 100 max, regex ext .(pdf|docx?|odt|xlsx?|
     pptx?|jpg|png|webp|zip) ou keyword telechargement|download|attachment
     dans l'href.

Messages :
  'aurora-verify-auth' { authMarkers[] } → 1.5s settle puis return
    { ok, url, looksLogin, errFound, markersFound, score }.
  'aurora-scrape-page' → return { snapshot, attachments, hostname, ts }.

Le 1.5s settle laisse le temps aux SPAs (React/Vue/Angular)
d'hydrater le DOM post-redirect.

Bump version 1.0.5 → 1.0.6.

_2026-05-03 · `fd6a888`_

────────────── application/extension/vault.js (NEW, 175 L) ──────────────

Aurora-Connect credentials vault. Les creds ne quittent JAMAIS
l'appareil — chiffrés AES-GCM 256-bit, passphrase dérivée PBKDF2
200k iter SHA-256.

Storage : chrome.storage.local (jamais sync, jamais bridge).

API exposée :
  vault.unlock(passphrase) → derive key + cache en mémoire.
    Throw si passphrase < 6 chars.
  vault.lock() → wipe in-memory key (reset à chaque restart extension).
  vault.list() → siteKey[] sans déchiffrer (pour UI listing).
  vault.add(siteKey, loginUrl, username, password) → encrypt + store.
  vault.get(siteKey) → { loginUrl, username, password } déchiffré.
    Throw si decrypt fail (mauvaise passphrase).
  vault.remove(siteKey).
  vault.wipeAll() → factory reset.

Format storage chrome.storage.local :
  aurora_vault_v1: {
    salt: base64,                  // généré 1× à install
    entries: {
      [siteKey]: {
        loginUrl, iv: base64, ciphertext: base64,
        createdAt, lastUsed
      }
    }
  }

Exposé en self.auroraVault (service worker) ET window.auroraVault
(popup). Utilisable via chrome.runtime.sendMessage.

────────────── Sécurité ──────────────

- Salt 16 bytes random à install. Persisté en clair (OK car
  PBKDF2 200k iter sur passphrase costaud reste impractical
  hors-ligne).
- IV 12 bytes random par entry (AES-GCM nonce best practice).
- Key in-memory only, wiped au lock() ou restart extension.
- Decrypt fail → message "passphrase incorrecte ?" pour user.

Bump extension version 1.0.2 → 1.0.3 → reload auto.

### `v82jo` — Pass A · fix skin switch white screen + auto-bump extension version

_2026-05-03 · `4d75010`_

User reports :
  1. "quand je passe a l'autre mode visuel elle recharge mais reste blanche"
  2. "fait en sorte que si tu dois modifier aurora extension alors avec
     aurora update ça met a jour également"

────────────── 1. Fix skin switch white screen ──────────────

Cause : le service worker (notifications) cache les chunks JS de
l'ancien skin. Au reload après skin switch, le navigateur sert les
vieux chunks qui ne matchent plus le boot-script data-ui-skin →
React mismatch + page blanche.

Fix dans SettingsPanel.pickUiSkin :
  - await caches.keys() + Promise.all(caches.delete(k)) avant reload.
  - Force navigation vers URL avec query string ?skin=X&_t=timestamp
    pour cache-buster index.html (et donc nouveaux module preloads).
  - Fallback window.location.reload() si URL API fail.

Plus : EntDashboard typeof Notification check pour éviter crash
en private browsing / contexts sans Notification API.

────────────── 2. Auto-bump extension version ──────────────

Nouveau bump-extension-version.py :
  - SHA256 de tous les fichiers de application/extension/.
  - Compare au .extension-hash.txt stocké à la racine.
  - Si différent : bump manifest.json patch version + write hash.

Intégration update-aurora-prod.bat :
  - Étape [0b/7] python bump-extension-version.py.
  - Renumérotation toutes étapes /6 → /7.

Le bridge sert déjà /api/cowork/extension/version qui lit le manifest.
L'extension polle cet endpoint et déclenche chrome.runtime.reload()
automatique quand version change → auto-update sans clic user.

Test live : version 1.0.1 → 1.0.2 (hash 65765a60).

────────────── Vérification ──────────────

Build prod 1.37s. Smoke 7/7 GO.

────────────── What was done (Pass A) ──────────────
  - pickUiSkin clear caches + URL cache-buster.
  - EntDashboard Notification typeof guard.
  - bump-extension-version.py SHA256-based.
  - update-aurora-prod.bat étape 0b ajoutée.

────────────── Plan loop Phase 3 + bonus (~10 passes) ──────────────

Pass 1/10 : Vault chrome.storage AES-GCM credentials extension.
Pass 2/10 : Popup UI add/edit/delete entries vault.
Pass 3/10 : Content-script auto-fill login form.
Pass 4/10 : Verify auth post-submit (markers + URL change).
Pass 5/10 : Bridge endpoint LLM-based DOM analyzer.
Pass 6/10 : Service entCredentialBridge.ts orchestrator.
Pass 7/10 : UI EntCredentialPanel Settings (consent + status).
Pass 8/10 : Extension scrape adaptatif post-auth.
Pass 9/10 : Edge cases (CAPTCHA, MFA, session timeout).
Pass 10/10 : Polish + integration test + final récap.

Possibilité +1-2 passes si bugs / améliorations en chemin.

### `v82jn` — Pass 10 (bonus) · fix extension "se déconnecte" + spec credential auto-login

_2026-05-03 · `bbc97b5`_

User report :
  "je vois pas de aurora extension dans les paramètres pour vérifier que
   c'est connecter mais d'ailleurs faut absolument pas elle se déconnecte"
  +
  "faut trouver juste le moyen de scrapping différent y'a pas d'ical mais
   au pire il donne le mot de passe et le nom d'utilisateur et aurora
   extension va sur le site de connexion se connecte capture et analyse"

────────────── 2 problèmes diagnostiqués ──────────────

PROBLÈME 1 — extension perçue comme "déconnectée"
Cause : _EXT_LAST_SEEN persistait sur disk seulement 5 min. Quand bridge
self-watch reload (chaque save de bridge_server.py = respawn), l'extension
disparaissait pendant ~25s en attente du prochain poll. Pendant les
9 passes Phase 2 j'ai modifié bridge_server.py 5×, donc à chaque fois
l'extension était absente ~25s.

Fix bridge_server.py :
  _ext_persist_load() : window 300s → 86400s (24h).
  Le 120s filter côté list reste pour distinguer "active maintenant"
  vs "connue mais en attente". Mais le persist long permet de garder
  la mémoire que l'extension EST installée même si elle ne poll pas
  pendant quelques minutes.

Nouveau endpoint GET /api/cowork/extension/status :
  Retourne { active[], activeCount, persisted[], persistedCount,
    totalKnown } avec lastSeenAgoMs par item.
  active = vues <120s. persisted = vues entre 120s et 24h.

────────────── ExtensionStatusPanel (UI Settings) ──────────────

Refactor pour utiliser /status au lieu de /list :
  - Polling 15s (était 30s) pour reactivity.
  - 3 états distincts au lieu de 2 :
    ✓ vert : actif maintenant ("✓ N onglets actifs · il y a Xs")
    ⚠ jaune : connue mais en attente ("⚠ N extensions connues, en attente
       · vue il y a Xmin")
    gris muted : jamais détectée
  - fmtAge helper (s/min/h/j).
  - Bouton ↻ force re-test.
  - Hint contextuel "ouvre un onglet pour qu'elle re-poll" si persisted
    seulement.

────────────── PROBLÈME 2 — scraping différent (sans iCal) ──────────────

User insight : pas tous les ENT ont iCal export. Solution proposée :
Aurora-Connect prend username + password, va sur la page login, fill
+ submit + scrape. "niveau de compréhension élevé".

Spec complète écrite dans :
_design/pronote_phase3_credential_autologin.md (200 L)

5 étapes architecture :
  1. Vault popup extension : chrome.storage.local + AES-GCM
     window.crypto.subtle, passphrase maître par appareil, jamais
     transmise au bridge.
  2. Auto-fill content script : detect inputs username/password,
     fill, click submit, wait DOMContentLoaded.
  3. Verify auth : URL change + absence error markers + presence des
     authMarkers de l'adapter (réutilise entAdapters Phase 2).
  4. Scrape adaptatif via LLM : DOM textuel (innerText 30 KB) +
     outerHTML containers → POST bridge → Ollama avec prompt système
     extraction JSON structurée. Plus de selectors hardcodés.
  5. Erreurs : detect CAPTCHA / 2FA / session expired → notif user
     pour intervention manuelle.

Sécurité & légal documentés :
  - Disclaimer obligatoire au activation.
  - Opt-in (checkbox "j'accepte").
  - Notice ToS ENT.
  - Bouton "Oublier toutes les infos".

Estimation Phase 3 : 5-8 jours focused dev.

────────────── Vérification ──────────────

Build prod 1.38s. Smoke 7/7 GO.

Endpoint /api/cowork/extension/status testé live :
  {"active":[{"extId":"ext-1777318406885-r2m09n","lastSeenAgoMs":1047}],
   "activeCount":1,"persisted":[],"persistedCount":0,"totalKnown":1}
→ extension réelle de l'user détectée immédiatement après bridge respawn.

Tunnel verified : status endpoint répond 200 avec extension visible.

────────────── What was done ──────────────
  - Bridge persist TTL 24h (était 5 min).
  - Endpoint /api/cowork/extension/status (active + persisted).
  - ExtensionStatusPanel UI claire avec 3 états + age formatté.
  - Phase 3 spec complète (credential auto-login).
  - Build prod + smoke + tunnel verify.

────────────── What remains (Phase 3 — quand l'user dira "go") ──────────────

  - Vault popup extension (chrome.storage + crypto.subtle).
  - Content scripts auto-fill / scrape.
  - Bridge endpoint LLM-based DOM analyzer.
  - UI Settings credential consent.

### `v82jk` — Pass 7/9 · ENT notif scheduler (X jours avant éval, toggleable)

_2026-05-03 · `38b2ada`_

Phase 2 P2.6 — schedule des notifications X jours avant chaque éval
détectée. Notifications Web (déjà autorisées via SettingsPanel push
permission v60+).

────────────── application/src/services/entNotifScheduler.ts (NEW, 130 L) ──────────────

API :
  readNotifPrefs() / writeNotifPrefs(prefs) → { enabled, leadDays }
    persisté localStorage 'aurora-ent-notif-prefs-v1'.
    leadDays ∈ {1, 3, 7}, default 3.

  recomputeEvalNotifs(devoirs[]) → { scheduled, alreadySent, pastDue, notEval }
    - Skip si !enabled OR Notification.permission != 'granted'.
    - Clear all active timeouts avant.
    - Pour chaque devoir : fastClassify → si isEval :
      - Skip si date passée.
      - Compute notifAt = dueDate - leadDays*24h.
      - Skip si déjà sent (lookup 'aurora-ent-notifs-sent-v1').
      - setTimeout pour fire au notifAt → new Notification(...) +
        record sent + dispatch CustomEvent 'aurora-academy-focus-eval'
        au onclick (l'UI Academy Pass 9 écoute pour scroll).
    - Cap max delay à 2_147_000_000ms (~24.8j, setTimeout int32 max).
    - Cleanup sent records >30j pour storage non-saturated.

  triggerTestNotif() → bool
    Force notif test pour vérifier que la permission marche.

  getNotifStats() → { activeTimeouts, sentTotal }
    Pour UI Settings.

Active timeouts tracked en Map module-scope pour pouvoir cancel
clean au re-compute (sinon doublons garantis).

### `v82jj` — Pass 6/9 · ENT eval detector (fast regex + LLM fallback)

_2026-05-03 · `31a02ac`_

Phase 2 P2.5 — détection automatique évaluation/DM/devoir/leçon à
partir du title+description scraped.

────────────── application/src/services/entEvalDetector.ts (NEW, 165 L) ──────────────

Pipeline 2 niveaux :

1. fastClassify(item) — regex/keyword instant, no LLM.
   18 patterns ordonnés par spécificité :
     - eval : DST, DS, contrôle, évaluation, interro, test, examen,
       partiel, bac, oral, QCM (confidence 0.85-0.95).
     - dm : DM noté/à rendre (0.7-0.9).
     - devoir : "exo à faire" / "à rendre" (0.7).
     - lecon : "apprendre/réviser leçon" (0.7-0.8).
     - lecture : "lire/lecture" (0.6).
     - autre : default (0.3).
   isEval=true si kind='eval' OU (kind='dm' && conf>=0.85).

2. llmClassify(item) — Ollama fallback pour les ambigus.
   System prompt FR strict JSON {kind, confidence}.
   Type-guard sur réponse, fallback 'autre' si parse fail.

3. classifyEval(item, {llmThreshold=0.65}) — combine.
   Si fast confiant >= threshold OU fast='autre' (vide), skip LLM.
   Sinon escalade LLM.

4. classifyAll(items[], {maxLlmCalls=10}) — bulk.
   Fast pour tous, LLM seulement les top-10 ambigus.
   Promise.all pour parallel LLM calls.

Patterns inclusifs sur français scolaire (DM, DS, DST, interro, etc.)
+ tournures d'instruction ("à rendre", "à savoir", "à apprendre").

────────────── Vérification ──────────────

Build prod 1.36s. Smoke 7/7 GO.

────────────── What was done ──────────────
  - 18 patterns regex EVAL_PATTERNS.
  - fastClassify pure-compute.
  - llmClassify Ollama strict JSON.
  - Pipeline avec threshold + bulk caps.

### `v82ji` — Pass 5/9 · ENT notes trend analysis (priority ranking matières à remonter)

_2026-05-03 · `efcdab9`_

Phase 2 P2.4 — analyse trend des notes par matière.

────────────── application/src/services/entNotesAnalysis.ts (NEW, 130 L) ──────────────

API :
  normalizeGrade(grade, scale?) → ratio /20
    - Si scale présent, divise et multiplie par 20.
    - Sinon heuristique : <=20 supposé /20, <=100 supposé %.
  groupBySubject(notes[]) → Record<subject, sorted notes[]>
  subjectTrend(subject, notes[]) → SubjectTrend
  analyzeAllSubjects(notes[]) → SubjectTrend[] sorted priority desc
  formatTrend(t) → "Maths: 12.3/20 ▼ (-1.5 vs classe)"

SubjectTrend fields :
  studentAvg, classAvg, gapVsClass, slope (linear regression),
  direction ('up' | 'down' | 'flat' | 'unknown'), recentNormalized
  (last 6 notes /20), priority (0-100).

Priority scoring (0-100) :
  + max(0, (12 - studentAvg) * 5)    [0-50 : niveau absolu]
  + min(30, abs(gapVsClass) * 10)    [0-30 : sous-classe]
  + min(20, abs(slope) * 30)         [0-20 : trend down]

Le LLM Pass 8 (parcours révision) prend les top-3 matières et
génère cours+exos+flashcards focus sur celles-ci.

────────────── Vérification ──────────────

Build prod 1.36s. Smoke 7/7 GO.

Module pure-compute, pas de side effects, testable unitairement
plus tard.

### `v82jh` — Pass 4/9 · ENT doc parser PDF/DOCX → LLM JSON structured

_2026-05-03 · `a69ed8f`_

Phase 2 P2.3 — extraction texte + classification structurée des cours/
exos/devoirs harvested via Aurora-Connect.

────────────── application/src/services/entDocParser.ts (NEW, 130 L) ──────────────

Types :
  DocKind = 'cours' | 'exo' | 'devoir' | 'corrige' | 'fiche-revision' | 'autre'
  DocLevel = '6e' → 'Tle' | 'inconnu'
  DocStructured : { kind, subject, chapter, level, keyPoints[], exercises[], abstract }

Pipeline :
  extractTextFromBlob(blob, mime?) → string
    - PDF : pdfjs-dist (déjà bundlé), 50 pages max, slice 50KB
    - DOCX : mammoth (déjà bundlé), extractRawText, 50KB
    - HTML : strip tags + collapse whitespace
    - TXT : direct text() decode
    Fallback fakeworker si pdfjs worker indispo.

  classifyDocument(text, hint?) → DocStructured | null
    - System prompt FR strict JSON (kind/subject/chapter/level/keyPoints/
      exercises/abstract).
    - Sample 8KB pour budget context LLM raisonnable.
    - Hint optionnel (filename, subject pré-détecté).
    - Match regex JSON object dans réponse (fallback si LLM ajoute prose
      malgré la consigne).
    - Validation type-guard sur chaque champ.

  parseAndClassify(blob, hint?) → { text, structured }
    - Combine extract + classify.
    - Skip LLM si text < 50 chars (doc vide/corrompu).

Liste matières FR couvertes : Maths, Physique-Chimie, SVT, Histoire-Géo,
  Français, Anglais, Espagnol, Allemand, Philosophie, NSI, SES, EMC,
  EPS, Arts.

### `v82jg` — Pass 3/9 · ENT harvest pipeline (bridge endpoints + frontend service+store)

_2026-05-03 · `2cb5477`_

Phase 2 P2.2 — pipeline harvest pour persister les items extraits par
Aurora-Connect content-script (Pronote/ÉcoleDirecte/Skolengo/...).

────────────── application/bridge_server.py ──────────────

3 endpoints REST sous /api/ent/* :

  POST /api/ent/harvest
    Body : { adapter, section, hostname, items[], ts? }.
    Persiste output/ent/{adapter}/{section}.json avec adapter/section/
    hostname/ts/count/items.
    Cap 1000 items max par harvest (anti-abuse).
    Adapter+section sanitized (alnum + - _, max 40 chars).
    Retour : { ok, count, path }.

  GET /api/ent/list?adapter=&section=
    Liste tous les harvests persistés. Filters optionnels.
    Retour : { ok, harvests: [{adapter, section, ts, count, hostname}] }.

  GET /api/ent/get?adapter=...&section=...
    Lit un harvest complet (avec items[]).
    Retour : { ok, data: payload | null }.

Helpers internes :
  _ent_dir(adapter, section) — path normalize + ensure dirs.

### `v82jc` — Pronote / portails école · proxy bridge ICS + finder UI 5 portails

_2026-05-03 · `c79686b`_

User report : bouton Sync ICS Pronote → "Failed to fetch" alors
que l'URL est valide. Cause : CORS — le navigateur bloque tout
fetch cross-origin sur les serveurs ENT (Pronote, ÉcoleDirecte,
Skolengo bloquent tous Access-Control-Allow-Origin pour HTTPS).
Plus, demande d'un finder par établissement + ville + icons.

────────────── application/bridge_server.py ──────────────

Nouveau endpoint POST /api/calendar/fetch-ics :
  - Body { url: string }.
  - Validate scheme http(s).
  - requests.get timeout 15s + UA "Aurora-Calendar-Bridge/1.0"
    + Accept "text/calendar".
  - Stream chunks 64kB cap 5 MB (anti-abuse).
  - Décode utf-8 (errors=replace).
  - Validate "BEGIN:VCALENDAR" présent → sinon error.
  - Catch Timeout / ConnectionError séparément pour message clair.

Le serveur Python n'a pas de CORS (server-to-server), donc Pronote
répond bien la ressource ICS publique de l'EDT.

────────────── application/src/components/SettingsPanel.tsx ──────────────

fetchFeed() refactor :
  - Try via /api/calendar/fetch-ics POST first.
  - Fallback direct fetch si bridge indispo (legacy compat,
    ne sera utilisé que si bridge mort — rare).
  - Si proxy retourne ok=false avec error explicite, propage
    le message tel quel.

Nouveau composant SchoolPortalsPanel :
  - 5 portails ENT FR : Pronote / ÉcoleDirecte / Skolengo /
    ENT Île-de-France / Google Calendar.
  - Chacun avec icon + label + finderUrl(query) + hint.
  - Champ "Établissement + ville" persisté localStorage
    'aurora-school-finder-query-v1'.
  - Click portail → ouvre new tab avec finderUrl(query)
    (prompt si query vide pour Pronote/Skolengo qui exigent une
    recherche).
  - Show hint contextuel après click pour expliquer "où trouver
    l'URL ICS dans ce portail".
  - Polling 30s extension Aurora-Connect : si non détectée,
    banner warning gold (pas erreur, juste info) expliquant que
    le scraping auto ne marchera pas.
  - onPickUrl prop pour permettre futur "auto-fill" depuis
    un click direct (pas câblé pour l'instant).

Inséré au-dessus du panneau "Importer un fichier .ics" dans tab
calendar.

### `v82j` — real WebGL2 sphere for V1 Editorial chat (no more flat gradient)

_2026-05-01 · `dd67b55`_

The V1 chat used to mount AuroraSpherePlaceholder — a flat CSS radial
gradient with a CSS pulse animation. The user's hard rule for this
rollout is "le design et pas l'écriture", and the centrepiece visual of
the Editorial design IS the sphere — state-driven simplex 3D noise,
rim light, halo, eased amp/speed transitions between idle/thinking/
streaming/voice. The placeholder lost all of that.

This commit ports `_design/aurora_design_lib/aurora-sphere.jsx` to
`application/src/components/AuroraSphereV1.tsx` :

- WebGL2 fragment shader (simplex 3D noise + lambert + rim) preserved
  byte-for-byte, retyped for TS.
- Canvas2D fallback (4-layer offset radial gradients) when WebGL2 is
  unavailable — same animation language, no graceful loss of "is alive".
- Empty canvas if even 2D context is missing — layout still flows.
- State targets eased (amp += (target - amp) * 0.04 per frame), so
  voice ↔ streaming ↔ idle transitions look organic.
- Tint accepts oklch() string OR hex; the converter is a port of the
  design lib's `oklchToRgb`.

`AuroraV1ChatView.tsx` swaps the placeholder for `<AuroraSphereV1
tint="oklch(0.72 0.120 200)" radius={0.42} glow={1.2} state=…/>`,
matching the design source's exact parameters.

No TS regression (error count unchanged at 38 pre-existing). HMR over
tunnel is live (v82i fix), Vite hot-loaded the new module.
